# MS.990 Parallel Edition: Canvases 485–514

## Page 540 — IIIF Canvas #485

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0485.JP2` (5704 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0485.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0485.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,950

Recipes for an incarnative ointment and astringent powder for open sores, accompanied by a watercolor illustration of ulcerated and bandaged lower legs, followed by red-ink rubricated clinical observations on hot phlegmonous leg ulcers, a cataplasm, litharge ointment, oak-ash lye wound water, and a red-ink-headed powder and plaster recipe.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Lower-leg ulceration and bandaging on a seated patient** (`#xywh=400,400,2152,1000`): Rectangular watercolor and ink illustration in the left column on a pale ochre ground showing the bare lower legs and feet of a seated figure wearing red breeches tucked above the knees. The right leg (viewer's left) displays scattered red pustules and small open sores on the shin and calf; the left leg (viewer's right) is wrapped around the mid-calf with a dark grey-blue cloth bandage, with red sores visible above and below the bandage.

## Page 451 [541] — IIIF Canvas #486

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0486.JP2` (6041 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0486.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0486.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,050

Recipes for a birthwort-tea-amalgam wound water, a calamine-litharge-lapis medicamentosus plaster, a watercolor illustration of an ulcerated lower leg resting on a wooden footstool, a red-ink heading on putrid and callous ulcers, and recipes for aqua phagedaenica and herbal-antimonial wound washes and powders.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Ulcerated lower leg resting on a wooden footstool** (`#xywh=400,400,2320,1000`): Rectangular watercolor and ink illustration in the left column on a pale ochre ground depicting a bare lower leg from above the knee to the foot, resting horizontally with the heel supported on a small four-legged wooden stool and the thigh resting on a round wooden block. Multiple dark red and brownish ulcerative lesions are visible along the shin, ankle, and upper foot.

## Page 542 — IIIF Canvas #487

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0487.JP2` (5729 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0487.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0487.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

Surgical recipes and treatise on old, malignant leg ulcers and sores ('Von Alten üblen Schäden Und geschweer an Painen'), including plasters, cataplasms, fomentations, and a section on cancerous/phagedenic ulcers ('Die Geschweer Von einer Vmb sich fressentem...'). Features a watercolor illustration of a leg with red sores resting on a wooden block.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**** (`#xywh=400,400,2164,1000`): Rectangular framed watercolor illustration in the middle of the left column depicting a bare human lower leg and foot resting the heel on a cylindrical wooden block on a greenish-yellow ground, with multiple reddish-brown circular lesions and ulcers along the shin and calf.

## Page 543 — IIIF Canvas #488

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0488.JP2` (5972 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0488.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0488.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,520

Continuation of recipes for cancerous/phagedenic ulcers and superfluous flesh, followed by a treatise and remedies for frostbitten feet and hands ('Erfrore Fieß', 'Für das beißen Und schmertz der Füeß Und hend', 'Wan man Sich Erfrert'). Features a watercolor illustration of a bandaged lower leg resting on a wooden block.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Anfang.** (`#xywh=400,400,2286,1000`): Rectangular watercolor illustration in the lower left column depicting a bare leg bent at the knee resting on a low yellow-ochre wooden block, with the lower leg tightly wrapped in a dark greyish-blue bandage and the bare foot extending to the right.

## Page 544 — IIIF Canvas #489

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0489.JP2` (5733 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0489.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0489.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,380

Conclusion of the frostbite section, followed by short recipes for foot ailments ('Vor das gefranß der Füeß', 'Vor mietigkeit der Füeß', 'Vor die aufgesprungne Fersen') and the beginning of a treatise on cramps and convulsions ('Khram. Spamus. Conuulsion'). Features a watercolor illustration of a nude man suffering a convulsion on a bed.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**242** (`#xywh=400,400,2166,1000`): Rectangular watercolor illustration at the bottom of the left column depicting a nude man lying on a yellow-ochre bed with a white bolster/pillow, his limbs and torso tensed in a convulsive spasm, with a dark backdrop and a brownish-green landscape to the right; numbered '242' in faint red-brown ink at the upper left of the frame.

## Page 545 — IIIF Canvas #490

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0490.JP2` (5987 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0490.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0490.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 3,450

Continuation of prognostic signs for cramps/spasms (when fatal after wounds, purging, dysentery, hellebore, or fevers), followed by a section on curing the cramp condition ('Chur über disen Chram Zuestands') with internal decoctions, purging pills, liniments/ointments, a home mineral-style bath ('Bad ex ▽.'), aromatic waters, and powders for cramps from cold, phlegm, or evacuation.

## Page 546 — IIIF Canvas #491

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0491.JP2` (5685 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0491.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0491.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 3,520

Left column continues cramp regimen (keeping the patient warm and in the dark), outlines a digestive and laxative cure, uterine extension/suffocation cramps, and clyster recipes. Right column features a framed watercolor illustration of a bedridden patient beneath 'Stirbt so stirbt der Patient —', followed by a new section 'Contractura.' discussing bilious and tartaric causes of limb contracture and initial purgative/antimony tincture treatment.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Contractura.** (`#xywh=400,400,2142,1000`): Framed rectangular watercolor and ink illustration at the top of the right column, beneath the line 'Stirbt so stirbt der Patient —' and above the large rubricated heading 'Contractura.'. It depicts a patient wearing a striped headscarf/nightcap, propped up on white pillows in a wooden bed with a carved headboard and footboard. The patient's upper torso is bare and the lower body is covered by a green/teal blanket over white bedsheets.

## Page 547 — IIIF Canvas #492

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0492.JP2` (5972 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0492.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0492.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 3,780

Full-width single-column page continuing the treatment of contracture ('Contractura'). Includes a gentle purgative infusion, an ordinary medicinal drink ('Volgentes Trankh Soll hir ordinarij geben Werden.'), and a series of therapeutic baths and herbal/metallic washes for contracted, lame, and painful limbs ('Folgen die Bäder...', 'Ein Anders. für Contracte glider...', 'Ein Anders für Lame Glider.', 'Ein Bad in großen glider schmerzen', 'Ein Bad die Glider zu stärkhen'), concluding with the start of 'Köstliches Wasser in der Contractur'.

## Page 548 — IIIF Canvas #493

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0493.JP2` (5719 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0493.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0493.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

Continuation of a guaiacum and rhubarb electuary/decoction recipe with an ointment for evening application; followed by a section on Tremor (trembling of the hands or limbs), its causes, prognosis, pills for trembling (castoreum, asafoetida, colocynth, centaury), an electuary (Volgente Lattwerung) with myrobalans and spices, remedies for trembling caused by mercury/quicksilver or poisonous ores (theriac, masterwort, angelica, sweating and herbal baths), and the start of a proven ointment and bath for lame limbs.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |

## Page 549 — IIIF Canvas #494

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0494.JP2` (6006 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0494.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0494.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Continuation of the 18-day bath, ointment (Die Salben Mach also), and medicinal wine drink (Volgentes Tranck Solle der Krancke Trincken) for lame limbs; followed by a major section on Phlegmon or inflammation of a limb (Von der phlegmone oder Entzündung eines Glidts), its anatomical nomenclature (ophthalmia, angina, pleuritis, erysipelas, panaritium, furunculus, anthrax, ranula, buboes, parotis), causes involving volatile salt, bloodletting, and purgatives (resin of jalap).

## Page 550 — IIIF Canvas #495

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0495.JP2` (5734 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0495.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0495.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,400

Continuation of internal and external remedies for phlegmon/inflammation (including antimony, sal ammoniac, laudanum, spirit of hartshorn, spermaceti, diaphoretic antimony, pike's jawbone, unicorn horn, and volatile-salt herbs), followed by external resolvents vs. repellents, and a new section on cold watery swellings or oedema (Von Kalten Wäserigen Geschwulsten. oedema), prescribing diet, guaiacum/sassafras/colocynth decoction, and sweating.

## Page 551 — IIIF Canvas #496

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0496.JP2` (6004 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0496.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0496.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,650

Continuation of medical recipes for swellings, tumors, and foot/knee complaints, including decoctions of guaiacum and sassafras, lye washes with rock salt and quicksilver, a cerate for the knee, and cataplasms using cow/pigeon dung and mercurial water.

## Page 552 — IIIF Canvas #497

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0497.JP2` (5676 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0497.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0497.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,700

Conclusion of recipes for swellings (liniments and cataplasms), followed by an illustrated section 'Von Schwinden der Glider' (On the Wasting/Atrophy of the Limbs) with a colored drawing of a gesturing man, and recipes for baths, mixtures, and ointments for wasting limbs.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Anfang. / Von Schwinden der Glider** (`#xywh=400,400,2138,1000`): Colored pen-and-ink illustration in the lower-left column showing a half-length figure of a bearded man with shoulder-length brown hair, wearing a pinkish-red doublet with green-and-gold frogging/braiding and a white collar, standing behind a rectangular parapet or ledge. His right hand rests on his chest while his left arm is extended to the right, gesturing toward the opposite column. Below the ledge on the left is a small rectangular cartouche inscribed 'Anfang.' ('Beginning.'), with folio number '246' written in the left margin above the ledge.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | send als er die füeß mit warmen tüchern abtrück | so that he dries the feet with warm cloths |

## Page 553 — IIIF Canvas #498

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0498.JP2` (5971 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0498.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0498.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,750

Further ointments and a plaster for wasting limbs, a theoretical explanation of post-wound limb atrophy (loss of nourishing humor and corruption of the three principles), and an alchemical preparation of feather alum with rectified mercury followed by an ointment and plaster.

## Page 554 — IIIF Canvas #499

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0499.JP2` (5661 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0499.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0499.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,150

Medical/surgical treatise on rose-fistulas and ulcers (Von denen Rosen fistlen Vnd Geschweren), their causes, diagnostic signs (including bone caries and joint involvement), palliative vs. curative treatment, and recipes for plasters, injections, and washes. Top-left features a portrait of a young woman.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**aufgehört.** (`#xywh=400,400,2130,1000`): Half-length watercolor-tinted ink portrait of a young woman facing forward/slightly left, with brown hair in a braided updo, wearing a red coral necklace, a laced red-and-grey/blue bodice over a white puffed-sleeve chemise, and raising her left hand with a red, black-spotted cuff. She stands behind a horizontal fluted parapet/ledge inscribed 'aufgehört.' in brown ink.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | Von denen Rosen fistlen Vnd | Of the rose-fistulas and *— Note: Rubricated heading in red ink* |
| 2 | Geschweren. | ulcers. *— Note: Rubricated heading in red ink* |
| 3 | Auß dem, auß die glider fallende flei / Vd hume= | From the phlegm and humors falling onto the limbs, |
| 4 | res. werde geschwächte Vd Entzündte Schärffest | there arise weakened and inflamed sharpest [ulcers] |
| 5 | auf den Endzündten. Welche geschwär Vd apostem | upon the inflamed [parts]. Which ulcers and apostemes |
| 6 | auß dem geschweren werde fistlen, Vd Vntheils | from the ulcers become fistulas, and partly |
| 7 | Sein ganz gefährliche Vd lang wirige schädē, ob n | are very dangerous and long-lasting injuries, whether now |
| 8 | endliche am ellichen bey den Junghfräuen So Vil | finally on the elbows among young maidens so many |
| 9 | Bein Vd aderen seindt. | bones and sinews/veins are. |
| 10 | die gefahr aber man deratione Erkenen, | The danger, however, one recognizes by reason, |
| 11 | die geschwär. Je mehr beisente mitl man brau= | the ulcers. The more biting remedies one must |
| 12 | muß. Zum Exempel, das Vng: apostolorum Vd | use. For example, the Unguentum Apostolorum and |

## Page 555 — IIIF Canvas #500

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0500.JP2` (6046 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0500.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0500.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,300

Continuation of fistula and ulcer treatments, featuring recipes for medicated waters, ointments (including Unguentum Basilicum and the 'factotum Magistri'), plasters for skull ulcers near the periosteum, and instructions for preparing a pressed wax-and-resin bath-sponge tent (illustrated in the right column) to dilate narrow fistulas and treat callosities.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**** (`#xywh=400,400,2323,1000`): Hand-drawn, yellow-tinted watercolor illustration of a porous, two-lobed bath sponge (Badeschwamm) with dark ink stippling representing pores, positioned in the upper-middle right column beside the recipe for preparing a medicated sponge tent.

## Page 556 — IIIF Canvas #501

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0501.JP2` (5744 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0501.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0501.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,250

Section on carious bones (Cariosi Baines), detailing surgical access, scraping/searing, and powders/infusions (including euphorbium, birthwort, guaiacum, iris, and calcined chalk) to treat rotten bones and draw out splinters; followed by a new rubricated section on oil-fillers/balsams (Von Etlichen Oel Füllner), beginning with 'the Black Lion' (der Schwarze Leb). Includes illustrations of a bare foot resting on a block and a standing yellow lion.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**** (`#xywh=400,400,2172,1000`): Upper left: Watercolor-tinted ink illustration of a bare human lower leg and foot resting on a rectangular wooden block/stone on a grassy ground, accompanying the section on carious bones (Cariosi Baines).

**** (`#xywh=400,400,2172,1000`): Lower left: Watercolor-tinted ink illustration of a standing golden-yellow lion facing left with a flowing mane and curled tail on brownish-green ground, accompanying the recipe 'Ein füllner der Schwarze Leb genandt' ('A filler/balsam called the Black Lion').

## Page 557 — IIIF Canvas #502

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0502.JP2` (6029 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0502.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0502.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 3,100

Page 557 (verso) contains corrosive and caustic powder and ointment recipes for fistulas, eye fistulas, and foul wounds, including a rubricated recipe titled 'Der Graue Wolff' (The Grey Wolf) illustrated with a painted wolf, as well as several further corrosive ointments ('Egents Sälbl', 'Egentte Salbn', 'Ezung ohne Schmerzen').

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Ein anders der Graue Wolff genandt.** (`#xywh=400,400,2314,1000`): Colour-washed pen-and-ink illustration of a grey-brown wolf standing in profile facing left on a patch of ochre-green ground, positioned in the upper-left column beside the recipe ingredients for 'Der Graue Wolff'.

**tz Puluär Gar kröfftig** (`#xywh=400,400,2314,1000`): Small painted reddish-brown oval diagram (representing a medicinal pellet or troche) placed inline between the two halves of the rubricated heading 'tz Puluär' and 'Gar kröfftig' in the right column.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0503.JP2` (5765 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0503.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0503.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 3,050

## Page 559 — IIIF Canvas #504

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0504.JP2` (6038 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0504.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0504.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 3,150

Page 559 (verso) concludes the flesh-growing powder from p. 558, gives instructions for testing precipitated mercury on a coal, and introduces a new surgical section titled 'Von Angeloffnen Schbarzen painern' (On swollen black/necrotic bones) illustrated with a seated figure pointing to a cautery/scraping instrument near the leg.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Von Angeloffnen Schbarzen painern** (`#xywh=400,400,2319,1000`): Colour-washed pen-and-ink illustration in the middle of the left column showing the lower body and legs of a seated figure wearing a yellow doublet, pinkish-red breeches, and a black shoe, extending their right leg forward and pointing with the left hand toward a small metal surgical instrument (cautery iron or bone scraper) resting on the ground near the ankle.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0505.JP2` (5667 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0505.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0505.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,450

Surgical and pharmaceutical recipes for fluxes, corrupted flesh, and wound-healing powders (including preparations with mercury, litharge, camphor, terra sigillata, Armenian bole, tutia, and burnt lead), followed by a colored illustration of a pointing hand and a section in red ink on treating a whitlow/felon ('Von dem Wurm an finger') and preparing precipitated mercury ('precipitat pp.').

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Pointing hand (top of right column)** (`#xywh=400,400,2133,1000`): Colored ink and wash illustration at the top of the right column depicting a right hand with the index finger extended horizontally to the right, resting above a double horizontal blue/teal ink rule.

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0506.JP2` (6056 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0506.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0506.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,520

Continuation of the preparation of dulcified mercury precipitate ('☿ precipitat. dulcinicirt'), followed by a colored illustration of a man firing a musket and a rubricated section on gunshot wounds ('Von den Geschoßnen Wunden'), including oleum catellorum (oil of puppies), salves, and powders for extracting gunpowder and shot from wounds.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Man firing a musket (Von den Geschoßnen Wunden)** (`#xywh=400,400,2328,1000`): Colored ink and wash illustration in the middle of the left column depicting a bearded man in profile facing left, wearing a wide-brimmed dark hat with a feather and a green-and-ochre doublet, aiming and firing a long musket/arquebus to the left with a puff of grey smoke and red-orange flame rising from the firing pan, above a horizontal tan ledge.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0507.JP2` (5652 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0507.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0507.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,540

Further treatments for gunpowder embedded in the skin, internal fever remedies, eye washes and collyria for burns/gunpowder in the eyes, a burn salve ('brandsalbel'), and simpler household remedies for burns from water, lard, oil, or glue.

## Page 503 — IIIF Canvas #508

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0508.JP2` (6057 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0508.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0508.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Early New High German medical and apothecary recipes (verso, p. 503) in two columns. The left column continues treatments for burns and gangrene (using frog spawn, green willow bark in butter, egg white with prepared tutty, rose oil, linseed oil, unslaked lime, and litharge). The right column contains three rubricated recipes in red ink for frostbitten hands and feet in winter, swelling of the feet, and a precious medicinal water (with mercury sublimate) for rising, swelling, ulcers, and scabby eruptions of the feet and hands.

## Page 564 — IIIF Canvas #509

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0509.JP2` (5718 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0509.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0509.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,650

Surgical treatise 'Von fistlen in Gemain' (Of fistulas in general), accompanied by a watercolor illustration of a seated man with red lesions/fistulas on his face, arms, and legs. The text discusses probing fistulas to assess depth and hidden passages, widening openings with tent dilators (Stellmeißel), deciding between surgical excision/cauterization and palliative care near major nerves and blood vessels, and supporting local treatment with strict diet, purgatives, decoctions, and wound potions.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**[Untitled illustration of a man afflicted with fistulas/lesions]** (`#xywh=400,400,2159,1000`): Rectangular watercolor illustration framed in red ink in the upper half of the left column. It depicts a man with shoulder-length brown hair wearing a pinkish-tan tunic with green collar, cuffs, and a green sash, seated or crouching on green-and-ochre grassy ground with low green bushes to the left. His left arm is extended to the right and his bare legs are stretched forward; his face, arms, hands, and legs are covered with numerous small red spots representing fistulas or ulcers. Faint, faded markings appear near the upper border of the illustration.

## Page 565 — IIIF Canvas #510

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0510.JP2` (6029 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0510.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0510.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Early New High German medical/apothecary manuscript (Wellcome MS.990, p. 565, verso). The left column concludes a treatment note and presents a rubricated section on the signs and diagnosis of fistulas (distinguishing marrow, nerve/membrane, flesh, blood-vessel, and carious-bone fistulas, followed by dietary and caustic treatment advice). The right column begins a rubricated section on fistulas of the breast/chest, followed by four Latin-German apothecary recipes: a precious plaster for fistulas with preparation instructions, two salves/liniments, and a caustic wash for spongy flesh in fistulas.

## Page 566 — IIIF Canvas #511

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0511.JP2` (5723 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0511.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0511.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Recto folio 566 (pencil foliation 253) containing the conclusion of a fistula treatment, two recipes for wound drinks for fistulas ('Wundt Trankh in den fistlen' and 'Ein anders Trankh in den fistlen'), general surgical advice on purging, diet, bandaging, cutting, and cauterizing stubborn fistulas, and a large watercolor illustration of seven surgical instruments (two curved fistula knives, two straight bistouries/probes, surgical scissors, and three cautery irons).

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**diser Vnder glich Instrumenta ~** (`#xywh=400,400,2161,1000`): Large watercolor illustration occupying the lower half of the page, depicting seven surgical instruments for treating fistulas: two curved sickle-shaped bistouries/syringotomes with slender probe tips at the top; a straight probe/director with a turned dark wooden handle; a straight probe-pointed bistoury with a light brown wooden handle; a pair of surgical shears/scissors with looped spring-like handles; and three long-shafted actual cautery irons with turned wooden handles and variously shaped tips (triangular/hatchet, bulbous/olivary, and flat disc).

### Full Page

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | 566 | 566 *— Note: Boxed page number in upper right corner (with faint '19' or '12' below)* |

## Page 568 — IIIF Canvas #513

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0513.JP2` (5666 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0513.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0513.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Recto folio 568 (pencil foliation 254) in two-column layout, featuring a watercolor illustration of a patient reclining in bed covered with red fistula sores/pustules, followed by 'Von den fistlen des Vndern Leibs' (On fistulas of the lower body), a cataplasm recipe, and several fistula/wound-water recipes ('Ein khöstliches Waser in den fistlen', 'Ein anders', 'Waser zuo den fistlen darmit zuo Rainigen ohne Schmerzen', 'Khöstliches Wundt Waser').

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Patient suffering from fistulas/sores (above 'Von den fistlen des Vndern Leibs')** (`#xywh=400,400,2133,1000`): Watercolor illustration in the upper left column showing a figure with brown curly hair lying half-reclining on a wooden bed with white sheets and pillow, uncovered to the thighs, with numerous red spots/ulcers/fistulas across the chest, abdomen, arms, and legs. Beyond the bed to the right is a green-and-ochre landscape with trees.

## Page 569 — IIIF Canvas #514

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0514.JP2` (6038 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0514.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0514.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

A two-column recto page (p. 569) containing a rubricated recipe for 'Ein Wunderbahrliches Waser' (A Miraculous Water) that cures fistulas by distilling turpentine, frankincense, aloe, mastic, galangal, saffron, cubebs, ivy gum, nutmeg, cinnamon, and cloves into three fractions (aqua balsami, oleum balsamum, balsamo artificialiter), followed by instructions on treating fistulas on the head, neck, and in Neapolitan disease (syphilis) with purgatives, wound drinks, and caustic/mercury-based applications.
